Conclusion: LVSpec通过区分视觉相关锚点和视觉无关填充内容，结合位置偏移容忍机制，成功实现了视频大语言模型的高效推测解码，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低了推理延迟，为视频理解任务提供了实用的加速解决方案。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) excel in video understanding but suffer from high inference latency during autoregressive generation. Speculative Decoding (SD) mitigates this by applying a draft-and-verify paradigm, yet existing methods are constrained by rigid exact-match rules, severely limiting the acceleration potential. To bridge this gap, we propose LVSpec, the first training-free loosely SD framework tailored for Video-LLMs. Grounded in the insight that generation is governed by sparse visual-relevant anchors (mandating strictness) amidst abundant visual-irrelevant fillers (permitting loose verification), LVSpec employs a lightweight visual-relevant token identification scheme to accurately pinpoint the former. To further maximize acceptance, we augment this with a position-shift tolerant mechanism that effectively salvages positionally mismatched but semantically equivalent tokens. Experiments demonstrate that LVSpec achieves high fidelity and speed: it preserves >99.8 of target performance while accelerating Qwen2.5-VL-32B by 2.70x and LLaVA-OneVision-72B by 2.94x. Notably, it boosts the mean accepted length and speedup ratio by 136% and 35% compared to SOTA training-free SD methods for Video-LLMs.

</details>


### [41] [LLM Reasoning as Trajectories: Step-Specific Representation Geometry and Correctness Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05655)
*Lihao Sun,Hang Dong,Bo Qiao,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将大语言模型的思维链生成表征为表示空间中的结构化轨迹，发现数学推理会穿越功能有序的步骤特定子空间，且正确与错误解在后期阶段系统性地分叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解大语言模型在数学推理任务中思维链生成的内在机制，探索推理过程在表示空间中的几何结构，以及如何利用这种结构来预测和控制模型的推理行为。

Method: 方法包括：1）将思维链生成表征为表示空间中的轨迹；2）分析数学推理过程中步骤特定子空间的功能有序性和可分离性；3）研究基础模型与推理训练对轨迹结构的影响；4）分析正确与错误解的轨迹分叉模式；5）开发基于轨迹的引导框架，用于推理修正和长度控制。

Result: 结果显示：1）数学推理穿越功能有序的步骤特定子空间，且可分离性随层深度增加；2）这种结构在基础模型中已存在，推理训练主要加速向终止相关子空间的收敛；3）正确与错误解在推理后期系统性地分叉；4）基于中期推理轨迹可预测最终答案正确性（ROC-AUC达0.87）；5）基于轨迹的引导框架能有效修正推理和控制推理长度。

Conclusion: 结论表明推理轨迹为解释、预测和控制大语言模型推理行为提供了一个几何视角，揭示了思维链生成的结构化本质，并为推理过程的干预和优化提供了新方法。

Abstract: This work characterizes large language models' chain-of-thought generation as a structured trajectory through representation space. We show that mathematical reasoning traverses functionally ordered, step-specific subspaces that become increasingly separable with layer depth. This structure already exists in base models, while reasoning training primarily accelerates convergence toward termination-related subspaces rather than introducing new representational organization. While early reasoning steps follow similar trajectories, correct and incorrect solutions diverge systematically at late stages. This late-stage divergence enables mid-reasoning prediction of final-answer correctness with ROC-AUC up to 0.87. Furthermore, we introduce trajectory-based steering, an inference-time intervention framework that enables reasoning correction and length control based on derived ideal trajectories. Together, these results establish reasoning trajectories as a geometric lens for interpreting, predicting, and controlling LLM reasoning behavior.

</details>


### [42] [Attention Editing: A Versatile Framework for Cross-Architecture Attention Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05688)
*Zhen Cheng,Hao-Bo Yang,Wan-Yi Huang,Jin-Long Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Attention Editing框架，无需从头预训练即可将已训练大语言模型转换为新注意力架构，显著提升长上下文推理效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存内存和带宽在长上下文和长生成场景下主导大语言模型推理成本，现有多头潜在注意力(MLA)和混合滑动窗口注意力(SWA)等架构难以集成到已有模型中，先前方法对源和目标注意力模块有细粒度结构要求，不满足实际部署需求

Method: 提出Attention Editing框架：1) 用可学习目标模块替换原始注意力；2) 渐进蒸馏训练：层间教师强制优化配合中间激活监督防止冷启动误差累积，模型级蒸馏优化下一词分布，可选弱特征匹配正则化；实例化为MLA和GateSWA(门控混合SWA)两种目标架构

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-30B-A3B模型上应用，转换后模型保持竞争力同时提供显著效率提升，证明大规模注意力转换可行且鲁棒；在Ascend 910B集群上完成实验，提供国产硬件上的实用训练案例研究

Conclusion: Attention Editing框架为已训练大语言模型集成新注意力架构提供实用解决方案，无需从头预训练，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理效率，为长上下文应用提供可行路径

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) cache memory and bandwidth increasingly dominate large language model inference cost in long-context and long-generation regimes. Architectures such as multi-head latent attention (MLA) and hybrid sliding-window attention (SWA) can alleviate this bound, but integrating them into existing models remains difficult. Prior methods impose fine-grained structural requirements on both source and target attention modules, which cannot meet the feasible requirement in practical deployment. We present Attention Editing, a practical framework for converting already-trained large language models (LLMs) with new attention architectures without re-pretraining from scratch. Attention editing replaces the original attention with a learnable target module and trains it using progressive distillation, consisting of (1) layer-wise teacher-forced optimization with intermediate activation supervision to prevent cold-start error accumulation, and (2) model-level distillation on next-token distributions, optionally regularized by weak feature matching. We instantiate the framework on two different target--MLA and GateSWA, a gated hybrid SWA design, and apply it to Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-30B-A3B. The resulting models maintain competitive performance while delivering substantial efficiency improvements, demonstrating that large-scale attention conversion is both feasible and robust. Notably, experiments are conducted on an Ascend 910B clusters, offering a practical training case study on domestic hardware.

</details>


### [43] [Dialogue Act Patterns in GenAI-Mediated L2 Oral Practice: A Sequential Analysis of Learner-Chatbot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05702)
*Liqun He,Shijun,Chen,Mutlu Cukurova,Manolis Mavrikis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析了9年级中国EFL学习者与生成式AI语音聊天机器人互动中的对话行为模式，发现高进步会话中学习者发起更多提问，而低进步会话中澄清请求更多，提示理解困难；高进步会话还表现出更多基于提示的纠正反馈序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然生成式AI语音聊天机器人为第二语言口语练习提供了可扩展的机会，但与学习者进步相关的互动过程仍未得到充分探索。本研究旨在通过分析学习者与AI聊天机器人互动中的对话行为模式，揭示有效互动的特征。

Method: 研究对12名9年级中国EFL学习者与生成式AI语音聊天机器人在10周干预期间的70个会话进行人工标注，使用基于教学法的编码方案，共标注6,957个对话行为。比较了高进步和低进步会话在对话行为分布和序列模式上的差异。

Result: 在对话行为层面，高进步会话中学习者发起的提问更多，而低进步会话中澄清请求的比例更高，表明理解困难更大。在序列层面，高进步会话表现出更频繁的基于提示的纠正反馈序列，这些序列始终位于学习者回应之后，突显了反馈类型和时机在有效互动中的作用。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了对话视角在生成式AI聊天机器人设计中的价值，贡献了一个基于教学法的对话行为编码框架，并为第二语言教育中自适应生成式AI聊天机器人的设计提供了信息。

Abstract: While generative AI (GenAI) voice chatbots offer scalable opportunities for second language (L2) oral practice, the interactional processes related to learners' gains remain underexplored. This study investigates dialogue act (DA) patterns in interactions between Grade 9 Chinese English as a foreign language (EFL) learners and a GenAI voice chatbot over a 10-week intervention. Seventy sessions from 12 students were annotated by human coders using a pedagogy-informed coding scheme, yielding 6,957 coded DAs. DA distributions and sequential patterns were compared between high- and low-progress sessions. At the DA level, high-progress sessions showed more learner-initiated questions, whereas low-progress sessions exhibited higher rates of clarification-seeking, indicating greater comprehension difficulty. At the sequential level, high-progress sessions were characterised by more frequent prompting-based corrective feedback sequences, consistently positioned after learner responses, highlighting the role of feedback type and timing in effective interaction. Overall, these findings underscore the value of a dialogic lens in GenAI chatbot design, contribute a pedagogy-informed DA coding framework, and inform the design of adaptive GenAI chatbots for L2 education.

</details>


### [44] [MedLayBench-V: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Expert-Lay Semantic Alignment in Medical Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05738)
*Han Jang,Junhyeok Lee,Heeseong Eum,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了首个大规模多模态基准MedLayBench-V，专注于医学影像理解的专家-患者语义对齐，通过结构化概念约束确保语义等价性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学视觉语言模型主要基于专业文献训练，缺乏向患者提供通俗解释的能力，而现有文本简化研究缺乏大规模多模态基准来支持患者可理解的医学影像理解

Method: 采用结构化概念锚定精炼(SCGR)流程，整合统一医学语言系统概念唯一标识符和微观实体约束，确保严格的语义等价性

Result: 构建了MedLayBench-V基准，为训练和评估能够弥合临床专家与患者沟通鸿沟的新一代医学视觉语言模型提供了验证基础

Conclusion: MedLayBench-V填补了医学多模态基准的关键空白，通过结构化概念约束方法确保专家术语与通俗解释的语义等价，为开发患者可理解的医学影像解释模型提供了重要资源

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) have achieved expert-level proficiency in interpreting diagnostic imaging. However, current models are predominantly trained on professional literature, limiting their ability to communicate findings in the lay register required for patient-centered care. While text-centric research has actively developed resources for simplifying medical jargon, there is a critical absence of large-scale multimodal benchmarks designed to facilitate lay-accessible medical image understanding. To bridge this resource gap, we introduce MedLayBench-V, the first large-scale multimodal benchmark dedicated to expert-lay semantic alignment. Unlike naive simplification approaches that risk hallucination, our dataset is constructed via a Structured Concept-Grounded Refinement (SCGR) pipeline. This method enforces strict semantic equivalence by integrating Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) Concept Unique Identifiers (CUIs) with micro-level entity constraints. MedLayBench-V provides a verified foundation for training and evaluating next-generation Med-VLMs capable of bridging the communication divide between clinical experts and patients.

</details>


### [45] [Controlling Distributional Bias in Multi-Round LLM Generation via KL-Optimized Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05756)
*Yanbei Jiang,Amr Keleg,Ryandito Diandaru,Jey Han Lau,Lea Frermann,Biaoyan Fang,Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的微调框架，通过结合引导令牌校准和语义对齐，使大语言模型能够生成符合目标分布的输出，解决了现有方法在分布对齐任务上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界本质上是随机的，但当前大语言模型主要针对单轮推理和固定真实值进行评估。需要评估LLM在重复提示下能否生成符合目标分布（如反映真实世界统计或均匀分布）的输出，特别是在性别、种族和情感等属性在职业背景下的分布对齐问题。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的微调框架，结合引导令牌校准与语义对齐。采用混合目标函数：使用KL散度锚定潜在引导令牌的概率质量，使用Kahneman-Tversky优化将这些令牌绑定到语义一致的响应。

Result: 在六个不同数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著优于基线模型，在属性生成任务中实现了精确的分布控制。现成的LLM和标准对齐技术（包括提示工程和直接偏好优化）无法可靠地控制输出分布。

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过引导令牌校准和语义对齐，成功解决了LLM在分布对齐任务中的局限性，为模型生成符合目标分布的输出提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While the real world is inherently stochastic, Large Language Models (LLMs) are predominantly evaluated on single-round inference against fixed ground truths. In this work, we shift the lens to distribution alignment: assessing whether LLMs, when prompted repeatedly, can generate outputs that adhere to a desired target distribution, e.g. reflecting real-world statistics or a uniform distribution. We formulate distribution alignment using the attributes of gender, race, and sentiment within occupational contexts. Our empirical analysis reveals that off-the-shelf LLMs and standard alignment techniques, including prompt engineering and Direct Preference Optimization, fail to reliably control output distributions. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel fine-tuning framework that couples Steering Token Calibration with Semantic Alignment. We introduce a hybrid objective function combining Kullback-Leibler divergence to anchor the probability mass of latent steering tokens and Kahneman-Tversky Optimization to bind these tokens to semantically consistent responses. Experiments across six diverse datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms baselines, achieving precise distributional control in attribute generation tasks.

</details>


### [46] [Identifying Influential N-grams in Confidence Calibration via Regression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05757)
*Shintaro Ozaki,Wataru Hashimoto,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究发现，尽管大语言模型在推理过程中会使用表达不确定性的语言表达，但其回答往往过于自信。通过回归分析识别出与置信度相关的特定语言表达，并发现可以通过抑制这些过度自信的表达来实现置信度校准，且不影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理过程中会表现出过度自信的问题，即使它们使用了表达不确定性的语言表达。研究者希望识别哪些具体的语言表达与模型的置信度相关，并探究这种过度自信行为背后的语言信息特征。

Method: 采用回归分析方法，将大语言模型推理部分中语言表达的置信度作为因变量，分析特定n-元语法与置信度之间的关系。研究在多个模型和问答基准上进行，并通过因果关系测试验证提取的语言信息是否真正影响置信度。

Result: 研究发现：1）大语言模型在涉及推理时仍然保持过度自信；2）这种过度自信行为可归因于特定的语言信息；3）提取的某些表达与测试时扩展中故意插入的提示短语重合；4）通过抑制这些过度自信的表达可以实现置信度校准，且不会导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的置信度校准可以通过简单地抑制过度自信的语言表达来实现，而不影响模型性能。这一发现为改进语言模型的置信度估计提供了实用方法，并揭示了语言表达与模型置信度之间的可解释关系。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) improve performance by explicit reasoning, their responses are often overconfident, even though they include linguistic expressions demonstrating uncertainty. In this work, we identify what linguistic expressions are related to confidence by applying the regression method. Specifically, we predict confidence of those linguistic expressions in the reasoning parts of LLMs as the dependent variables and analyze the relationship between a specific $n$-gram and confidence. Across multiple models and QA benchmarks, we show that LLMs remain overconfident when reasoning is involved and attribute this behavior to specific linguistic information. Interestingly, several of the extracted expressions coincide with cue phrases intentionally inserted on test-time scaling to improve reasoning performance. Through our test on causality and verification that the extracted linguistic information truly affects confidence, we reveal that confidence calibration is possible by simply suppressing those overconfident expressions without drops in performance.

</details>


### [47] [PhageBench: Can LLMs Understand Raw Bacteriophage Genomes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05775)
*Yusen Hou,Weicai Long,Haitao Hu,Houcheng Su,Junning Feng,Yanlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhageBench是首个评估噬菌体基因组理解的基准测试，包含5,600个高质量样本，涵盖筛选、质量控制、表型注释三个阶段的五个核心任务，评估发现通用推理模型在噬菌体基因组理解方面有潜力但存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噬菌体作为生物圈的"暗物质"在调节微生物生态系统和抗生素替代方面具有关键作用，但通用大语言模型在直接解释原始核苷酸序列和进行生物推理方面的能力尚未充分探索，需要专门的评估基准。

Method: 开发PhageBench基准测试，包含5,600个高质量样本，覆盖筛选、质量控制、表型注释三个阶段的五个核心任务，评估了八个大语言模型在噬菌体基因组理解方面的表现。

Result: 通用推理模型在噬菌体contig识别和宿主预测方面显著优于随机基线，显示出基因组理解的潜力，但在涉及长程依赖和细粒度功能定位的复杂推理任务中存在显著局限性。

Conclusion: 需要开发具有增强推理能力的下一代模型来处理生物序列，当前模型在复杂生物推理任务中仍有不足，PhageBench为评估和改进模型提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Bacteriophages, often referred to as the dark matter of the biosphere, play a critical role in regulating microbial ecosystems and in antibiotic alternatives. Thus, accurate interpretation of their genomes holds significant scientific and practical value. While general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at understanding biological texts, their ability to directly interpret raw nucleotide sequences and perform biological reasoning remains underexplored. To address this, we introduce PhageBench, the first benchmark designed to evaluate phage genome understanding by mirroring the workflow of bioinformatics experts. The dataset contains 5,600 high-quality samples covering five core tasks across three stages: Screening, Quality Control, and Phenotype Annotation. Our evaluation of eight LLMs reveals that general-purpose reasoning models significantly outperform random baselines in phage contig identification and host prediction, demonstrating promising potential for genomic understanding. However, they exhibit significant limitations in complex reasoning tasks involving long-range dependencies and fine-grained functional localization. These findings highlight the necessity of developing next-generation models with enhanced reasoning capabilities for biological sequences.

</details>


### [48] [What Models Know, How Well They Know It: Knowledge-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Learning When to Say "I Don't Know"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05779)
*Joosung Lee,Hwiyeol Jo,Donghyeon Ko,Kyubyung Chae,Cheonbok Park,Jeonghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过多采样推理估计细粒度实例级知识分数，基于此分数调整学习信号，鼓励模型对超出知识范围的问题明确回答"我不知道"，从而减少幻觉并改善不确定性表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在多样化用户查询中表现出强大能力，但仍存在幻觉问题，这通常源于预训练和微调之间的知识不对齐。需要解决这种不对齐，使模型能更准确地表达其知识边界。

Method: 通过多采样推理可靠地估计细粒度的实例级知识分数；利用知识分数根据模型现有知识缩放学习信号；鼓励模型对超出知识范围的查询明确回答"我不知道"。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法使模型在缺乏知识时能明确表达不确定性，同时保持对能回答问题的准确性。提出的不确定性评估指标显示，准确区分已知和未知实例能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 通过基于知识分数的学习信号缩放和鼓励明确的不确定性表达，可以有效减少大型语言模型的幻觉问题，改善模型对自身知识边界的认知和表达。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities across diverse user queries, they still suffer from hallucinations, often arising from knowledge misalignment between pre-training and fine-tuning. To address this misalignment, we reliably estimate a fine-grained, instance-level knowledge score via multi-sampled inference. Using the knowledge score, we scale the learning signal according to the model's existing knowledge, while encouraging explicit "I don't know" responses for out-of-scope queries. Experimental results show that this approach allows the model to explicitly express uncertainty when it lacks knowledge, while maintaining accuracy on questions it can answer. Furthermore, we propose evaluation metrics for uncertainty, showing that accurate discrimination between known and unknown instances consistently improves performance.

</details>


### [49] [CLEAR: Cross-Lingual Enhancement in Alignment via Reverse-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05821)
*Seungyoon Lee,Minhyuk Kim,Seongtae Hong,Youngjoon Jang,Dongsuk Oh,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLEAR提出了一种新的损失函数，通过反向训练方案增强跨语言检索性能，特别在低资源语言上提升显著（最高15%），同时最小化英语性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言嵌入模型面临两个主要问题：1）语言资源不平衡导致跨语言场景性能不佳；2）标准对比学习方法可能无法充分捕捉语言间的基本对齐，甚至可能损害英语等已对齐良好的语言性能。

Method: 提出CLEAR（Cross-Lingual Enhancement in Retrieval via Reverse-training）损失函数，采用反向训练方案，以英语段落为桥梁来增强目标语言与英语之间的对齐，从而提升跨语言检索任务的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明CLEAR在跨语言场景中取得显著改进，特别是在低资源语言上提升高达15%，同时最小化英语性能下降。此外，CLEAR在多语言训练中也表现出良好效果，显示出广泛应用的潜力。

Conclusion: CLEAR通过创新的反向训练方案有效解决了跨语言检索中的对齐问题，在提升低资源语言性能的同时保持英语性能稳定，具有广泛的应用前景和可扩展性。

Abstract: Existing multilingual embedding models often encounter challenges in cross-lingual scenarios due to imbalanced linguistic resources and less consideration of cross-lingual alignment during training. Although standardized contrastive learning approaches for cross-lingual adaptation are widely adopted, they may struggle to capture fundamental alignment between languages and degrade performance in well-aligned languages such as English. To address these challenges, we propose Cross-Lingual Enhancement in Retrieval via Reverse-training (CLEAR), a novel loss function utilizing a reverse training scheme to improve retrieval performance across diverse cross-lingual retrieval scenarios. CLEAR leverages an English passage as a bridge to strengthen alignments between the target language and English, ensuring robust performance in the cross-lingual retrieval task. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that CLEAR achieves notable improvements in cross-lingual scenarios, with gains up to 15%, particularly in low-resource languages, while minimizing performance degradation in English. Furthermore, our findings highlight that CLEAR offers promising effectiveness even in multilingual training, suggesting its potential for broad application and scalability. We release the code at https://github.com/dltmddbs100/CLEAR.

</details>


### [50] ["OK Aura, Be Fair With Me": Demographics-Agnostic Training for Bias Mitigation in Wake-up Word Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05830)
*Fernando López,Paula Delgado-Santos,Pablo Gómez,David Solans,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了人口统计信息无关的训练技术在减少唤醒词检测中人口统计偏见方面的有效性，通过数据增强和知识蒸馏技术显著降低了性别、年龄和口音方面的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音接口广泛应用，但唤醒词检测在不同说话人群中的公平性仍面临挑战，存在持续的人口统计偏见。需要开发有效方法来减少性别、年龄和口音等人口统计因素导致的性能差异。

Method: 使用OK Aura数据库进行实验，采用人口统计信息无关的训练方法（训练时不使用人口统计标签，仅用于评估）。探索两种技术：(i) 数据增强技术以提高模型泛化能力；(ii) 预训练基础语音模型的知识蒸馏。

Result: 人口统计信息无关的训练技术显著减少了人口统计偏见，在不同说话人群之间实现了更公平的性能表现。其中一项评估技术相比基线实现了：性别预测差异减少39.94%，年龄预测差异减少83.65%，口音预测差异减少40.48%。

Conclusion: 标签无关方法在促进唤醒词检测公平性方面具有显著效果，为减少语音技术中的人口统计偏见提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Voice-based interfaces are widely used; however, achieving fair Wake-up Word detection across diverse speaker populations remains a critical challenge due to persistent demographic biases. This study evaluates the effectiveness of demographics-agnostic training techniques in mitigating performance disparities among speakers of varying sex, age, and accent. We utilize the OK Aura database for our experiments, employing a training methodology that excludes demographic labels, which are reserved for evaluation purposes. We explore (i) data augmentation techniques to enhance model generalization and (ii) knowledge distillation of pre-trained foundational speech models. The experimental results indicate that these demographics-agnostic training techniques markedly reduce demographic bias, leading to a more equitable performance profile across different speaker groups. Specifically, one of the evaluated techniques achieves a Predictive Disparity reduction of 39.94\% for sex, 83.65\% for age, and 40.48\% for accent when compared to the baseline. This study highlights the effectiveness of label-agnostic methodologies in fostering fairness in Wake-up Word detection.

</details>


### [51] [AgentGL: Towards Agentic Graph Learning with LLMs via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05846)
*Yuanfu Sun,Kang Li,Dongzhe Fan,Jiajin Liu,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgentGL：首个基于强化学习的智能图学习框架，通过图感知导航和LLM推理的交替过程，显著提升图结构数据的理解能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架将外部信息视为非结构化文本，未能利用现实世界数据固有的拓扑依赖关系，限制了LLM在复杂关系环境中的推理能力

Method: 提出AgentGL框架：1) 为LLM智能体配备多尺度图探索工具；2) 通过搜索约束思维调节工具使用以平衡准确性和效率；3) 采用图条件课程强化学习策略稳定长时程策略学习

Result: 在多样化文本属性图基准测试中，AgentGL显著优于现有GraphLLMs和GraphRAG基线，节点分类绝对提升达17.5%，链接预测提升达28.4%

Conclusion: 智能图学习是使LLM能够自主导航和推理复杂关系环境的有前景的前沿方向，AgentGL为此提供了有效的强化学习驱动框架

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly rely on agentic capabilities-iterative retrieval, tool use, and decision-making-to overcome the limits of static, parametric knowledge. Yet existing agentic frameworks treat external information as unstructured text and fail to leverage the topological dependencies inherent in real-world data. To bridge this gap, we introduce Agentic Graph Learning (AGL), a paradigm that reframes graph learning as an interleaved process of topology-aware navigation and LLM-based inference. Specifically, we propose AgentGL, the first reinforcement learning (RL)-driven framework for AGL. AgentGL equips an LLM agent with graph-native tools for multi-scale exploration, regulates tool usage via search-constrained thinking to balance accuracy and efficiency, and employs a graph-conditioned curriculum RL strategy to stabilize long-horizon policy learning without step-wise supervision. Across diverse Text-Attributed Graph (TAG) benchmarks and multiple LLM backbones, AgentGL substantially outperforms strong GraphLLMs and GraphRAG baselines, achieving absolute improvements of up to 17.5% in node classification and 28.4% in link prediction. These results demonstrate that AGL is a promising frontier for enabling LLMs to autonomously navigate and reason over complex relational environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/sunyuanfu/AgentGL.

</details>


### [52] [LoRM: Learning the Language of Rotating Machinery for Self-Supervised Condition Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05863)
*Xiao Qin,Xingyi Song,Tong Liu,Hatim Laalej,Zepeng Liu,Yunpeng Zhu,Ligang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoRM是一个自监督框架，将旋转机械信号视为机器语言，通过令牌化多模态传感器数据并预测未来信号演化，实现实时状态监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统旋转机械状态监测方法依赖手工设计的变换和特征提取，缺乏通用性和适应性。作者提出将旋转机械信号视为机器语言，利用预训练语言模型的强大序列建模能力，为工业信号分析提供新范式。

Method: 1. 将多模态传感器数据重构为基于令牌的序列预测问题；2. 将每个数据窗口的观测上下文保留为连续形式，未来目标段量化为离散令牌；3. 通过部分微调通用预训练语言模型实现知识迁移；4. 通过跟踪令牌预测误差作为健康指标进行状态监测。

Result: 原位刀具状态监测实验表明，LoRM能够实现稳定的实时跟踪和强大的跨刀具泛化能力。该方法在工业信号分析中展示了实用价值，源代码已公开。

Conclusion: LoRM成功构建了语言建模与工业信号分析之间的实用桥梁，通过自监督框架实现了旋转机械多模态信号理解和实时状态监测，避免了从头训练大型模型的需求。

Abstract: We present LoRM (Language of Rotating Machinery), a self-supervised framework for multi-modal rotating-machinery signal understanding and real-time condition monitoring. LoRM is built on the idea that rotating-machinery signals can be viewed as a machine language: local signals can be tokenised into discrete symbolic units, and their future evolution can be predicted from observed multi-sensor context. Unlike conventional signal-processing methods that rely on hand-crafted transforms and features, LoRM reformulates multi-modal sensor data as a token-based sequence-prediction problem. For each data window, the observed context segment is retained in continuous form, while the future target segment of each sensing channel is quantised into a discrete token. Then, efficient knowledge transfer is achieved by partially fine-tuning a general-purpose pre-trained language model on industrial signals, avoiding the need to train a large model from scratch. Finally, condition monitoring is performed by tracking token-prediction errors as a health indicator, where increasing errors indicate degradation. In-situ tool condition monitoring (TCM) experiments demonstrate stable real-time tracking and strong cross-tool generalisation, showing that LoRM provides a practical bridge between language modelling and industrial signal analysis. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Q159753258/LormPHM.

</details>


### [53] [Understanding Performance Gap Between Parallel and Sequential Sampling in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05868)
*Xiangming Gu,Soham De,Larisa Markeeva,Petar Veličković,Razvan Pascanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 并行采样在大型推理模型中优于顺序采样，主要原因是顺序采样因条件依赖而缺乏探索性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在数学和编程等挑战性问题中表现出色，但需要多次采样才能获得高质量解决方案。虽然理论上顺序采样应具有更强的表示能力，但实践中并行采样表现更好，需要探究其根本原因。

Method: 提出三个假设验证并行采样优于顺序采样的原因：(1)聚合操作器优势；(2)顺序采样需要更长上下文；(3)顺序采样因条件依赖导致探索不足。在多种模型家族和规模（Qwen3、DeepSeek-R1蒸馏模型、Gemini 2.5）以及问题领域（数学和编程）上进行实证分析。

Result: 实证证据表明，聚合操作和上下文长度不是性能差距的主要原因。相比之下，探索不足起着更重要的作用，这是并行采样优于顺序采样的主要原因。

Conclusion: 顺序采样因条件依赖限制了探索性，导致性能不如并行采样。这一发现对设计更有效的推理采样策略具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have shown remarkable performance on challenging questions, such as math and coding. However, to obtain a high quality solution, one may need to sample more than once. In principal, there are two sampling strategies that can be composed to form more complex processes: sequential sampling and parallel sampling. In this paper, we first compare these two approaches with rigor, and observe, aligned with previous works, that parallel sampling seems to outperform sequential sampling even though the latter should have more representation power. To understand the underline reasons, we make three hypothesis on the reason behind this behavior: (i) parallel sampling outperforms due to the aggregator operator; (ii) sequential sampling is harmed by needing to use longer contexts; (iii) sequential sampling leads to less exploration due to conditioning on previous answers. The empirical evidence on various model families and sizes (Qwen3, DeepSeek-R1 distilled models, Gemini 2.5) and question domains (math and coding) suggests that the aggregation and context length do not seem to be the main culprit behind the performance gap. In contrast, the lack of exploration seems to play a considerably larger role, and we argue that this is one main cause for the performance gap.

</details>


### [54] [Mechanistic Circuit-Based Knowledge Editing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05876)
*Tianyi Zhao,Yinhan He,Wendy Zheng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MCircKE：一种基于机制电路的精确知识编辑框架，通过识别特定推理任务的因果电路并仅在该映射电路内进行外科手术式参数更新，解决多步推理中的"推理鸿沟"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实动态环境中部署大型语言模型时，需要更新其预训练知识。现有知识编辑方法虽然能可靠地修补孤立事实，但经常遭受"推理鸿沟"问题：模型能回忆起编辑后的事实，但在多步推理链中无法有效利用这些事实。

Method: MCircKE（机制电路知识编辑）采用"映射-适应"编辑程序：1）首先识别负责特定推理任务的因果电路，捕捉事实存储及其逻辑后果的路由；2）然后仅在该映射电路内进行外科手术式参数更新。

Result: 在MQuAKE-3K基准测试上的广泛实验证明了该方法在多跳推理知识编辑中的有效性。

Conclusion: MCircKE通过精确的电路级编辑，成功解决了知识编辑中的推理鸿沟问题，为大型语言模型在动态环境中的知识更新提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in real-world dynamic environments raises the challenge of updating their pre-trained knowledge. While existing knowledge editing methods can reliably patch isolated facts, they frequently suffer from a "Reasoning Gap", where the model recalls the edited fact but fails to utilize it in multi-step reasoning chains. To bridge this gap, we introduce MCircKE (\underline{M}echanistic \underline{Circ}uit-based \underline{K}nowledge \underline{E}diting), a novel framework that enables a precise "map-and-adapt" editing procedure. MCircKE first identifies the causal circuits responsible for a specific reasoning task, capturing both the storage of the fact and the routing of its logical consequences. It then surgically update parameters exclusively within this mapped circuit. Extensive experiments on the MQuAKE-3K benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method for multi-hop reasoning in knowledge editing.

</details>


### [55] [FRENCH-YMCA: A FRENCH Corpus meeting the language needs of Youth, froM Children to Adolescents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05899)
*Cherifa Ben Khelil,Jean-Yves Antoine,Anaïs Halftermeyer,Frédéric Rayar,Mathieu Thebaud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍了French-YMCA语料库，这是一个专门为儿童和青少年设计的法语语言资源，包含39,200个文本文件和22,471,898个单词，旨在训练适合年轻用户的语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童和青少年的语言需求独特，他们的语言技能处于不断发展中，与成人不同。需要专门的语言资源来支持开发适合这一年龄段用户的数字交互工具。

Method: 构建了French-YMCA语料库，包含39,200个文本文件，总计22,471,898个单词。该语料库具有多样化的来源、一致的语法和拼写，并承诺提供开放的在线访问。

Result: 创建了一个大规模、专门针对儿童和青少年的法语语料库，包含22,471,898个单词，具有多样化的文本来源和高质量的语言特性。

Conclusion: French-YMCA语料库可以作为训练理解青少年语言的语言模型的基础，从而提高数字交互质量，确保响应和建议适合年龄和理解水平。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the French-YMCA corpus, a new linguistic resource specifically tailored for children and adolescents. The motivation for building this corpus is clear: children have unique language requirements, as their language skills are in constant evolution and differ from those of adults. With an extensive collection of 39,200 text files, the French-YMCA corpus encompasses a total of 22,471,898 words. It distinguishes itself through its diverse sources, consistent grammar and spelling, and the commitment to providing open online accessibility for all. Such corpus can serve as the foundation for training language models that understand and anticipate youth's language, thereby enhancing the quality of digital interactions and ensuring that responses and suggestions are age-appropriate and adapted to the comprehension level of users of this age.

</details>


### [56] [FrontierFinance: A Long-Horizon Computer-Use Benchmark of Real-World Financial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05912)
*Michael Krumdick,Varshini Reddy,Shivani Chaudhary,William Day,Maarij Ahmed,Hayan Haqqi,Muhammad Ahsen Fahim,Hanzallah Amjad,Ahmad Orakzai,Aqsa Gul,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FrontierFinance是一个用于评估LLMs在复杂金融建模任务中表现的长期基准，包含25个跨5个核心金融模型的任务，每个任务平均需要超过18小时的专业人工完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在知识密集型行业（特别是金融领域）的劳动力替代风险日益加剧，但现有基准无法衡量实际专业专长任务的表现，且缺乏明确的问责机制。

Method: 与金融专业人士合作开发了包含25个复杂金融建模任务的基准，涵盖5个核心金融模型；创建了结构化评估的详细评分标准；聘请人类专家定义任务、制定评分标准、评估LLMs，并作为人类基准完成任务。

Result: 人类专家平均得分更高，且比当前最先进的系统更可能提供可直接交付给客户的输出结果。

Conclusion: FrontierFinance填补了金融领域AI评估的空白，为跟踪现实世界发展提供了稳健的基准，并揭示了当前LLMs在复杂专业任务中与人类专家仍存在差距。

Abstract: As concerns surrounding AI-driven labor displacement intensify in knowledge-intensive sectors, existing benchmarks fail to measure performance on tasks that define practical professional expertise. Finance, in particular, has been identified as a domain with high AI exposure risk, yet lacks robust benchmarks to track real-world developments. This gap is compounded by the absence of clear accountability mechanisms in current Large Language Model (LLM) deployments. To address this, we introduce FrontierFinance, a long-horizon benchmark of 25 complex financial modeling tasks across five core finance models, requiring an average of over 18 hours of skilled human labor per task to complete. Developed with financial professionals, the benchmark reflects industry-standard financial modeling workflows and is paired with detailed rubrics for structured evaluation. We engage human experts to define the tasks, create rubrics, grade LLMs, and perform the tasks themselves as human baselines. We demonstrate that our human experts both receive higher scores on average, and are more likely to provide client-ready outputs than current state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [57] ["I See What You Did There": Can Large Vision-Language Models Understand Multimodal Puns?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05930)
*Naen Xu,Jiayi Sheng,Changjiang Li,Chunyi Zhou,Yuyuan Li,Tianyu Du,Jun Wang,Zhihui Fu,Jinbao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MultiPun数据集和评估框架，系统研究视觉语言模型对多模态双关语的理解能力，发现现有模型难以区分双关语与对抗性非双关干扰项，并提出改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双关语是一种利用多义性和语音相似性创造幽默的修辞手法。在多模态双关中，视觉和文本元素协同作用，同时体现字面意义和比喻意义。尽管视觉语言模型在多模态理解和生成中广泛应用，但由于缺乏严格的基准测试，其理解双关语的能力尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 首先提出了多模态双关语生成流程，然后引入了MultiPun数据集，包含多种类型的双关语以及对抗性非双关干扰项。提出了提示级和模型级两种策略来增强双关理解能力。

Result: 评估显示大多数模型难以区分真正的双关语与对抗性干扰项。提出的改进方法使F1分数平均提高了16.5%。

Conclusion: 研究结果为开发未来能够通过跨模态推理掌握人类幽默微妙之处的视觉语言模型提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Puns are a common form of rhetorical wordplay that exploits polysemy and phonetic similarity to create humor. In multimodal puns, visual and textual elements synergize to ground the literal sense and evoke the figurative meaning simultaneously. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are widely used in multimodal understanding and generation, their ability to understand puns has not been systematically studied due to a scarcity of rigorous benchmarks. To address this, we first propose a multimodal pun generation pipeline. We then introduce MultiPun, a dataset comprising diverse types of puns alongside adversarial non-pun distractors. Our evaluation reveals that most models struggle to distinguish genuine puns from these distractors. Moreover, we propose both prompt-level and model-level strategies to enhance pun comprehension, with an average improvement of 16.5% in F1 scores. Our findings provide valuable insights for developing future VLMs that master the subtleties of human-like humor via cross-modal reasoning.

</details>


### [58] [BOSCH: Black-Box Binary Optimization for Short-Context Attention-Head Selection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05942)
*Abbas Ghaddar,Ivan Kobyzev,Boxing Chen,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BOSCH是一种无需训练的黑盒二元优化方法，用于在LLM后训练混合化中选择短上下文注意力头，通过大邻域搜索解决层级和静态头级方案的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM混合化方案存在局限性：层级方案忽略同一层内不同注意力头处理局部和全局依赖的能力差异；静态头级方案存在纠缠问题——头的局部/全局行为在混合化后可能改变。

Method: BOSCH将问题形式化为大邻域搜索，分解为三个子问题：1) 通过小预算黑盒探测进行层重要性检测；2) 基于敏感性自适应分配每层SWA比例；3) 在比例桶内进行分组头级优化。

Result: 在4个1.7B到30B参数的LLM和4种SWA比例上的实验表明，BOSCH始终优于层级启发式方法和6种强静态头级方法，在更高SWA比例下增益更大。在持续预训练中，BOSCH能更快、更高水平地恢复原始长上下文性能。

Conclusion: BOSCH选择的头在不同SWA比例下存在显著更替，强调了对每个目标比例进行头级选择的重要性，而非依赖固定的局部性排名。该方法为LLM混合化提供了有效的训练无关解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training hybridization of large language models (LLMs) often replaces quadratic self-attention with sliding-window attention (SWA) to reduce KV cache usage and improve latency. Existing hybridization schemes are typically defined either at the layer level (e.g., interleaving) or at the head level via static rankings from local to global. Layer-level schemes ignore that local and global dependencies are routed through heads within the same layer, while static head-level rankings suffer from entanglement: a head's local/global behavior can change after hybridization. We propose BOSCH, Black-box Binary Optimization for Short-context Head Selection, a training-free method that formulates the problem as a Large Neighborhood Search and decomposes it into three subproblems: (i) layer-importance detection via small-budget black-box probes, (ii) adaptive per-layer SWA-ratio assignment based on these sensitivities, and (iii) grouped head-level optimization within ratio buckets. Extensive experiments on 4 LLMs ranging from 1.7B to 30B parameters, across 4 SWA ratios, show that BOSCH consistently outperforms layer-level heuristics and 6 strong static head-level methods, with larger gains at higher SWA ratios. Under continual pretraining, BOSCH recover original long-context performance faster and to a higher level. Analysis of the selected heads reveals substantial turnover for BOSCH across different SWA ratios, underscoring the importance of performing head-level selection for each target ratio rather than relying on fixed locality rankings.

</details>


### [59] [The Model Agreed, But Didn't Learn: Diagnosing Surface Compliance in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05995)
*Xiaojie Gu,Ziying Huang,Weicong Hong,Jian Xie,Renze Lou,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了当前知识编辑方法存在的表面遵从现象：编辑模型仅在特定提示下模仿目标输出，而非真正修改内部记忆结构，同时递归编辑会导致认知不稳定和不可逆的记忆状态退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型内部化大量世界知识作为参数化记忆，但不可避免地继承了源语料库的陈旧性和错误。知识编辑提供了一种无需重新训练即可精确修改记忆的范式，但当前评估框架仅依赖特定提示条件下的输出评估，无法可靠验证真正的记忆修改是否发生。

Method: 引入一个简单的诊断框架，在上下文学习(ICL)设置下对模型进行判别性自我评估，该设置更好地反映了实际应用环境，专门设计用于检查记忆修改引起的微妙行为变化。

Result: 揭示了普遍存在的"表面遵从"现象：编辑器仅通过模仿目标输出获得高基准分数，而非结构性覆盖内部信念。此外，递归修改会积累表征残留，引发认知不稳定，并永久降低模型记忆状态的可逆性。

Conclusion: 当前编辑范式存在风险，强调了稳健记忆修改在构建可信赖、长期可持续的LLM系统中的关键作用。需要更严格的评估方法来确保真正的记忆修改而非表面遵从。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) internalize vast world knowledge as parametric memory, yet inevitably inherit the staleness and errors of their source corpora. Consequently, ensuring the reliability and malleability of these internal representations is imperative for trustworthy real-world deployment. Knowledge editing offers a pivotal paradigm for surgically modifying memory without retraining. However, while recent editors demonstrate high success rates on standard benchmarks, it remains questionable whether current evaluation frameworks that rely on assessing output under specific prompting conditions can reliably authenticate genuine memory modification. In this work, we introduce a simple diagnostic framework that subjects models to discriminative self-assessment under in-context learning (ICL) settings that better reflect real-world application environments, specifically designed to scrutinize the subtle behavioral nuances induced by memory modifications. This probing reveals a pervasive phenomenon of Surface Compliance, where editors achieve high benchmark scores by merely mimicking target outputs without structurally overwriting internal beliefs. Moreover, we find that recursive modifications accumulate representational residues, triggering cognitive instability and permanently diminishing the reversibility of the model's memory state. These insights underscore the risks of current editing paradigms and highlight the pivotal role of robust memory modification in building trustworthy, long-term sustainable LLM systems. Code is available at https://github.com/XiaojieGu/SA-MCQ.

</details>


### [60] [Disentangling MLP Neuron Weights in Vocabulary Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06005)
*Asaf Avrahamy,Yoav Gur-Arieh,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ROTATE是一种无需数据、无需前向传播的权重空间方法，通过最大化词汇空间峰度来解耦MLP神经元，恢复稀疏可解释的词汇通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型权重中编码的信息解释仍然是机制可解释性领域的基本挑战。现有方法通常需要数据或前向传播，限制了可扩展性和细粒度分析。

Method: 基于统计观察：编码连贯、单语义概念的神经元在投影到模型词汇空间时表现出高峰度。通过优化神经元权重的旋转以最大化其词汇空间峰度，恢复稀疏可解释的方向（词汇通道）。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Gemma-2-2B-it上的实验表明，ROTATE恢复的词汇通道忠实于神经元行为。消融单个通道能选择性地禁用相应输入激活或促进特定概念。通道级描述聚合产生的神经元描述在头对头比较中优于基于激活的基线方法2-3倍。

Conclusion: ROTATE通过提供神经元权重的无数据分解，为解释语言模型提供了一个可扩展、细粒度的构建模块。

Abstract: Interpreting the information encoded in model weights remains a fundamental challenge in mechanistic interpretability. In this work, we introduce ROTATE (Rotation-Optimized Token Alignment in weighT spacE), a data-free method requiring no forward passes that disentangles MLP neurons directly in weight space. Our approach relies on a key statistical observation: neurons that encode coherent, monosemantic concepts exhibit high kurtosis when projected onto the model's vocabulary. By optimizing rotations of neuron weights to maximize their vocabulary-space kurtosis, our method recovers sparse, interpretable directions which we name vocabulary channels. Experiments on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Gemma-2-2B-it demonstrate that ROTATE consistently recovers vocabulary channels that are faithful to the neuron's behavior. ablating individual channels selectively disables corresponding input activations or the promotion of specific concepts. Moreover, aggregating channel-level descriptions yields comprehensive neuron descriptions that outperform optimized activation-based baselines by 2-3x in head-to-head comparisons. By providing a data-free decomposition of neuron weights, ROTATE offers a scalable, fine-grained building block for interpreting LMs.

</details>


### [61] [BiMind: A Dual-Head Reasoning Model with Attention-Geometry Adapter for Incorrect Information Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06022)
*Zhongxing Zhang,Emily K. Vraga,Jisu Huh,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BiMind双头推理框架，通过解耦内容内部推理和知识增强推理来检测错误信息，引入注意力几何适配器、自检索知识机制和不确定性感知融合策略，并定义VoX指标量化知识贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有错误信息检测方法难以在文本内容验证和外部知识修正之间取得平衡，特别是在注意力几何结构崩溃的情况下。需要一种能够同时处理内容内部推理和知识增强推理的框架。

Method: 提出BiMind双头推理框架，包含三个核心创新：1) 注意力几何适配器，通过令牌条件偏移重塑注意力对数并缓解注意力崩溃；2) 自检索知识机制，通过kNN检索构建领域内语义记忆，并通过特征级线性调制注入检索到的邻居；3) 不确定性感知融合策略，包括熵门控融合和可训练一致性头，通过对称KL一致性正则化器稳定。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验结果表明，BiMind模型优于先进的检测方法，并能提供关于何时以及为何知识重要的可解释诊断。定义了VoX（经验价值）指标来量化知识增强推理带来的实例级对数增益。

Conclusion: BiMind框架通过解耦内容内部推理和知识增强推理，有效解决了错误信息检测中的平衡问题，提供了可解释的诊断能力，并通过VoX指标量化了知识贡献，为错误信息检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Incorrect information poses significant challenges by disrupting content veracity and integrity, yet most detection approaches struggle to jointly balance textual content verification with external knowledge modification under collapsed attention geometries. To address this issue, we propose a dual-head reasoning framework, BiMind, which disentangles content-internal reasoning from knowledge-augmented reasoning. In BiMind, we introduce three core innovations: (i) an attention geometry adapter that reshapes attention logits via token-conditioned offsets and mitigates attention collapse; (ii) a self-retrieval knowledge mechanism, which constructs an in-domain semantic memory through kNN retrieval and injects retrieved neighbors via feature-wise linear modulation; (iii) the uncertainty-aware fusion strategies, including entropy-gated fusion and a trainable agreement head, stabilized by a symmetric Kullback-Leibler agreement regularizer. To quantify the knowledge contributions, we define a novel metric, Value-of-eXperience (VoX), to measure instance-wise logit gains from knowledge-augmented reasoning. Experiment results on public datasets demonstrate that our BiMind model outperforms advanced detection approaches and provides interpretable diagnostics on when and why knowledge matters.

</details>


### [62] [A Multi-Stage Validation Framework for Trustworthy Large-scale Clinical Information Extraction using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06028)
*Maria Mahbub,Gregory M. Dams,Josh Arnold,Caitlin Rizy,Sudarshan Srinivasan,Elliot M. Fielstein,Minu A. Aghevli,Kamonica L. Craig,Elizabeth M. Oliva,Joseph Erdos,Jodie Trafton,Ioana Danciu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个多阶段验证框架，用于大语言模型（LLM）临床信息提取，无需密集人工标注即可实现可扩展、可信赖的评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在从非结构化健康记录中提取临床信息方面具有潜力，但在现实世界中的应用受到缺乏可扩展且可信赖的验证方法的限制。传统评估方法依赖密集标注的参考标准或不完整的结构化数据，难以在人群规模上实施。

Method: 提出多阶段验证框架，包括：提示校准、基于规则的合理性过滤、语义基础评估、使用独立高容量法官LLM进行针对性确认评估、选择性专家评审、外部预测有效性分析。该框架旨在量化不确定性并表征错误模式，无需大量人工标注。

Result: 应用于919,783份临床笔记中11种物质类别的物质使用障碍（SUD）诊断提取。基于规则的过滤和语义基础移除了14.59%的LLM阳性提取（不支持、不相关或结构不合理）。法官LLM评估与主题专家评审具有高度一致性（Gwet's AC1=0.80）。主要LLM在宽松匹配标准下F1分数为0.80。LLM提取的SUD诊断预测后续SUD专科护理参与比结构化数据基线更准确（AUC=0.80）。

Conclusion: 无需密集标注评估即可实现基于LLM的临床信息提取的可扩展、可信赖部署是可行的。该多阶段验证框架为在现实世界临床环境中部署LLM提供了实用且可扩展的评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for extracting clinically meaningful information from unstructured health records, yet their translation into real-world settings is constrained by the lack of scalable and trustworthy validation approaches. Conventional evaluation methods rely heavily on annotation-intensive reference standards or incomplete structured data, limiting feasibility at population scale. We propose a multi-stage validation framework for LLM-based clinical information extraction that enables rigorous assessment under weak supervision. The framework integrates prompt calibration, rule-based plausibility filtering, semantic grounding assessment, targeted confirmatory evaluation using an independent higher-capacity judge LLM, selective expert review, and external predictive validity analysis to quantify uncertainty and characterize error modes without exhaustive manual annotation. We applied this framework to extraction of substance use disorder (SUD) diagnoses across 11 substance categories from 919,783 clinical notes. Rule-based filtering and semantic grounding removed 14.59% of LLM-positive extractions that were unsupported, irrelevant, or structurally implausible. For high-uncertainty cases, the judge LLM's assessments showed substantial agreement with subject matter expert review (Gwet's AC1=0.80). Using judge-evaluated outputs as references, the primary LLM achieved an F1 score of 0.80 under relaxed matching criteria. LLM-extracted SUD diagnoses also predicted subsequent engagement in SUD specialty care more accurately than structured-data baselines (AUC=0.80). These findings demonstrate that scalable, trustworthy deployment of LLM-based clinical information extraction is feasible without annotation-intensive evaluation.

</details>


### [63] [Short Data, Long Context: Distilling Positional Knowledge in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06070)
*Patrick Huber,Ernie Chang,Chinnadhurai Sankar,Rylan Conway,Igor Fedorov,Md Rifat Arefin,Adithya Sagar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过logit-based知识蒸馏，可以在仅使用短上下文样本的情况下将长上下文检索能力迁移到学生模型，无需昂贵的长上下文预训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展语言模型上下文窗口通常需要昂贵的长上下文预训练，这给训练效率和数据收集带来重大挑战。本文旨在探索是否可以通过知识蒸馏将长上下文检索能力迁移到学生模型，从而避免直接的长上下文训练。

Method: 使用logit-based知识蒸馏方法，在长上下文窗口中仅使用打包的短上下文样本进行训练。通过Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE)的视角进行分析，采用phase-wise RoPE缩放策略，并设计了包含重复标记序列的实验设置来追踪位置信息传播。

Result: 1. phase-wise RoPE缩放在知识蒸馏设置中也实现了最佳的长上下文性能；2. logit-based知识蒸馏可以直接实现位置信息迁移，通过分析发现位置扰动从查询和键向量通过transformer层传播到输出logits；3. 揭示了长上下文扩展期间查询状态的结构化更新模式，特定参数跨度对长上下文训练表现出强敏感性。

Conclusion: 通过logit-based知识蒸馏可以在无需昂贵长上下文预训练的情况下将长上下文检索能力迁移到学生模型，这为高效扩展模型上下文窗口提供了新途径。

Abstract: Extending the context window of language models typically requires expensive long-context pre-training, posing significant challenges for both training efficiency and data collection. In this paper, we present evidence that long-context retrieval capabilities can be transferred to student models through logit-based knowledge distillation, even when training exclusively on packed short-context samples within a long-context window. We provide comprehensive insights through the lens of Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) and establish three key findings. First, consistent with prior work, we show that phase-wise RoPE scaling, which maximizes rotational spectrum utilization at each training stage, also achieves the best long-context performance in knowledge distillation setups. Second, we demonstrate that logit-based knowledge distillation can directly enable positional information transfer. Using an experimental setup with packed repeated token sequences, we trace the propagation of positional perturbations from query and key vectors through successive transformer layers to output logits, revealing that positional information systematically influences the teacher's output distribution and, in turn, the distillation signal received by the student model. Third, our analysis uncovers structured update patterns in the query state during long-context extension, with distinct parameter spans exhibiting strong sensitivity to long-context training.

</details>


### [64] [Stories of Your Life as Others: A Round-Trip Evaluation of LLM-Generated Life Stories Conditioned on Rich Psychometric Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06071)
*Ben Wigler,Maria Tsfasman,Tiffany Matej Hrkalovic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs能够基于真实心理测量数据生成具有人格特质的叙事文本，且独立LLMs能够从这些叙事中准确恢复人格分数，恢复效果接近人类测试-重测信度水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要依赖问卷自评、架构多样性有限，且很少使用真实人类心理测量数据，因此无法确定人格条件化是否真正产生具有心理测量信息性的个体差异表征，还是仅仅与特质描述符表面对齐。

Method: 使用290名参与者的真实心理测量档案条件化LLMs生成第一人称生活故事叙事，然后让独立的LLMs仅从这些叙事中恢复人格分数；评估了10个LLM叙事生成器和3个LLM人格评分器，涵盖6个提供商。

Result: 人格分数可以从生成的叙事中恢复，平均相关系数r=0.750，达到人类测试-重测信度上限的85%；恢复效果在10个LLM生成器和3个LLM评分器间保持稳健；内容分析显示人格条件化产生行为分化的文本，9/10编码特征与参与者真实对话中的相同特征显著相关。

Conclusion: 预训练中捕获的人格-语言关系支持个体差异的稳健编码和解码，包括在真实人类行为中复现的特征性情绪变异性模式，表明LLMs能够产生具有心理测量信息性的人格表征。

Abstract: Personality traits are richly encoded in natural language, and large language models (LLMs) trained on human text can simulate personality when conditioned on persona descriptions. However, existing evaluations rely predominantly on questionnaire self-report by the conditioned model, are limited in architectural diversity, and rarely use real human psychometric data. Without addressing these limitations, it remains unclear whether personality conditioning produces psychometrically informative representations of individual differences or merely superficial alignment with trait descriptors. To test how robustly LLMs can encode personality into extended text, we condition LLMs on real psychometric profiles from 290 participants to generate first-person life story narratives, and then task independent LLMs to recover personality scores from those narratives alone. We show that personality scores can be recovered from the generated narratives at levels approaching human test-retest reliability (mean r = 0.750, 85% of the human ceiling), and that recovery is robust across 10 LLM narrative generators and 3 LLM personality scorers spanning 6 providers. Decomposing systematic biases reveals that scoring models achieve their accuracy while counteracting alignment-induced defaults. Content analysis of the generated narratives shows that personality conditioning produces behaviourally differentiated text: nine of ten coded features correlate significantly with the same features in participants' real conversations, and personality-driven emotional reactivity patterns in narratives replicate in real conversational data. These findings provide evidence that the personality-language relationship captured during pretraining supports robust encoding and decoding of individual differences, including characteristic emotional variability patterns that replicate in real human behaviour.

</details>


### [65] [LAG-XAI: A Lie-Inspired Affine Geometric Framework for Interpretable Paraphrasing in Transformer Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06086)
*Olexander Mazurets,Olexander Barmak,Leonid Bedratyuk,Iurii Krak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LAG-XAI几何框架，将释义建模为嵌入空间中的仿射变换而非离散词替换，通过李群作用实现可解释的几何分解，在保持高性能的同时提供参数可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型虽然性能强大，但其潜在语义空间仍是难以解释的黑箱。需要一种数学基础扎实、资源高效的方法来实现Transformer的机制可解释性。

Method: 提出LAG-XAI框架，将释义概念化为语义流形上的连续几何流，采用受局部李群作用启发的计算高效平均场近似，将释义转换分解为旋转、变形和平移三个几何可解释分量。

Result: 在PIT-2015 Twitter语料上，仿射算子AUC达0.7713，捕获了约80%非线性基线的有效分类能力。发现稳定矩阵重配置角(~27.84°)和近零变形等几何不变量。在LLM幻觉检测中，通过"廉价几何检查"在HaluEval数据集上自动检测到95.3%的事实失真。

Conclusion: LAG-XAI为Transformer提供了数学基础扎实、资源高效的机制可解释性路径，通过几何框架实现了语义转换的显式参数化解释，在保持性能的同时大幅提升模型透明度。

Abstract: Modern Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance in natural language processing tasks, yet their latent semantic spaces remain largely uninterpretable black boxes. This paper introduces LAG-XAI (Lie Affine Geometry for Explainable AI), a novel geometric framework that models paraphrasing not as discrete word substitutions, but as a structured affine transformation within the embedding space. By conceptualizing paraphrasing as a continuous geometric flow on a semantic manifold, we propose a computationally efficient mean-field approximation, inspired by local Lie group actions. This allows us to decompose paraphrase transitions into geometrically interpretable components: rotation, deformation, and translation. Experiments on the noisy PIT-2015 Twitter corpus, encoded with Sentence-BERT, reveal a "linear transparency" phenomenon. The proposed affine operator achieves an AUC of 0.7713. By normalizing against random chance (AUC 0.5), the model captures approximately 80% of the non-linear baseline's effective classification capacity (AUC 0.8405), offering explicit parametric interpretability in exchange for a marginal drop in absolute accuracy. The model identifies fundamental geometric invariants, including a stable matrix reconfiguration angle (~27.84°) and near-zero deformation, indicating local isometry. Cross-domain generalization is confirmed via direct cross-corpus validation on an independent TURL dataset. Furthermore, the practical utility of LAG-XAI is demonstrated in LLM hallucination detection: using a "cheap geometric check," the model automatically detected 95.3% of factual distortions on the HaluEval dataset by registering deviations beyond the permissible semantic corridor. This approach provides a mathematically grounded, resource-efficient path toward the mechanistic interpretability of Transformers.

</details>


### [66] [Exclusive Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06154)
*Mutsumi Sasaki,Kouta Nakayama,Yusuke Miyao,Yohei Oseki,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Exclusive Unlearning方法，通过广泛遗忘除特定领域知识外的所有内容，实现大语言模型在工业应用中的广泛有害内容移除


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗、教育等工业应用中存在生成有害内容的风险，现有机器遗忘方法难以全面移除多样化的有害内容

Method: 提出Exclusive Unlearning方法，不单独列出需要遗忘的目标，而是广泛遗忘除希望保留的特定领域知识和表达之外的所有内容

Result: 通过Exclusive Unlearning可以获得对广泛输入（包括越狱攻击）保持安全的模型，同时保留对特定领域（如医学和数学）多样化指令的响应能力

Conclusion: Exclusive Unlearning方法能够实现广泛的有害内容移除，确保模型安全性，同时维持特定领域功能，为大语言模型在工业应用中的安全部署提供有效解决方案

Abstract: When introducing Large Language Models (LLMs) into industrial applications, such as healthcare and education, the risk of generating harmful content becomes a significant challenge. While existing machine unlearning methods can erase specific harmful knowledge and expressions, diverse harmful content makes comprehensive removal difficult. In this study, instead of individually listing targets for forgetting, we propose Exclusive Unlearning (EU), which aims for broad harm removal by extensively forgetting everything except for the knowledge and expressions we wish to retain. We demonstrate that through Exclusive Unlearning, it is possible to obtain a model that ensures safety against a wide range of inputs, including jailbreaks, while maintaining the ability to respond to diverse instructions related to specific domains such as medicine and mathematics.

</details>

### [67] [A column-generation approach for an electricity technician routing and scheduling problem with a lexicographic objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05153)
*Elise Bangerter,David Schindl,Meritxell Pacheco Paneque,Nour Elhouda Tellache,Rodolphe Griset*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对电力公司技术员调度问题，提出多目标优化方法，主要目标是最大化完成干预的总时长，次要目标是最小化运营成本，采用字典序优化，开发了基于列生成的精确算法，在5分钟内获得高质量解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力公司每天需要执行大量技术干预，但无法在一天内完成所有计划干预。公司面临两个目标：最大化完成干预的总时长（主要目标）和最小化相关运营成本（次要目标）。需要开发高效的调度算法来处理这两个按字典序排列的目标。

Method: 提出多目标技术员路径和调度问题变体，采用字典序优化。开发了紧凑的混合整数线性规划和扩展的基于集合覆盖的规划。为在单目标框架内处理多目标，考虑了保持字典序优先级的加权和重构，以及分别优化每个目标同时保持高优先级目标最优值的顺序重构。针对扩展规划，开发了基于列生成的精确算法，其中定价子问题通过基于动态规划的标记算法求解。算法设计为在短计算时间（如5分钟）内提供高质量解。

Result: 在法国电力公司提供的真实实例上的计算实验表明，基于列生成的算法在更多小实例上证明了最优性，并在较大实例上始终优于紧凑规划。特别是顺序基于列生成的变体在更多实例上找到了最佳已知解，并在每个实例类别中相对于找到的最佳解实现了更低的平均差距。

Conclusion: 提出的基于列生成的算法能够有效解决电力公司技术员调度中的多目标优化问题，在有限时间内提供高质量解，优于传统紧凑规划方法，适用于日常调度决策。

Abstract: Electric utility companies perform numerous technical interventions every day. Since it is generally not possible to complete all planned interventions within a single day, companies face two objectives: maximizing the total duration of completed interventions (primary objective) and minimizing the associated operational cost (secondary objective). In this paper, we introduce a multi-objective variant of the technician routing and scheduling problem in which both objectives are optimized in lexicographic order. We propose a compact mixed-integer linear formulation and an extended set-packing-based formulation. To handle the objectives within a single-objective framework, we consider weighted-sum reformulations that preserve lexicographic priorities as well as sequential reformulations that individually optimize each objective while maintaining the optimal value of higher-priority ones. For the extended formulation, we develop an exact column-generation-based algorithm, in which the pricing subproblems are solved via a labeling algorithm based on dynamic programming. As technician schedules are typically generated on a daily basis, the algorithm is designed to deliver high-quality solutions within short computation times (e.g., 5 minutes). Computational experiments on real-life instances provided by the French electric utility company show that the CG-based algorithm proves optimality on a larger number of small instances than the compact formulation and consistently outperforms it on larger instances. In particular, the sequential CG-based variant finds the best-known solutions on more instances and achieves lower mean gaps relative to the best solution found in each instance category.

</details>


### [68] [Controllability for semi-discrete semilinear stochastic parabolic operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05155)
*Rodrigo Lecaros,Ariel A. Pérez,Manuel F. Prado*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将φ-零能控性结果从线性随机抛物算子推广到半离散半线性随机算子，允许非线性项依赖一阶空间导数，并建立了新的Carleman估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管先前研究显示空间半离散化随机抛物算子在任意维度下通常不具有零能控性，但φ-零能控性结果仍然可达。本文旨在将这一结果扩展到包含一阶空间导数依赖的半离散半线性随机算子。

Method: 通过为伴随向后随机抛物算子建立新的Carleman估计，利用对偶论证获得线性系统的φ-零能控性，然后通过不动点论证处理半线性情况。

Result: 成功将φ-零能控性结果扩展到多维半离散半线性随机算子，其非线性项可依赖一阶空间导数，并统一了先前多个研究结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了半离散半线性随机抛物算子的φ-零能控性理论框架，为包含梯度依赖的非线性随机控制系统提供了新的分析工具和结果。

Abstract: In \cite{LPP:2025}, it was shown that, in arbitrary dimension, the spatial semi-discretization of a controlled stochastic parabolic operator is generically not null-controllable. Nevertheless, $φ$-null controllability results remain attainable. The present paper extends those results to semi-discrete semilinear stochastic operators in arbitrary dimension, whose nonlinearities may also depend on the first-order spatial derivatives. The approach relies on establishing a new Carleman estimate for the adjoint backward stochastic parabolic operator, which yields $φ$-null controllability for the associated linear system via a duality argument. The semilinear case is handeld by means of a fixed-point argument. As particular cases, our results recover the one-dimensional linear results of \cite{zhao:2024}, the multidimensional linear results of \cite{LPP:2025}, and the semilinear one-dimensional framework of \cite{WZ:2025} in the absence of gradient dependence.

</details>


### [69] [End-to-End Learning of Correlated Operating Reserve Requirements in Security-Constrained Economic Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05167)
*Owen Shen,Hung-po Chao,Haihao Lu,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种端到端可训练的鲁棒优化框架，用于学习电力系统中与可再生能源预测误差相关的储备需求，通过将相关储备集设计表述为可微优化问题，在满足覆盖要求的同时最小化调度成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全约束经济调度中的运行储备需求通常基于外部指定的可再生能源预测误差相关性结构，而不是从调度任务本身学习得到。这种外生设定可能导致次优的储备配置和更高的调度成本。

Method: 将相关储备集设计表述为端到端可训练的鲁棒优化问题：选择椭球不确定性集形状以最小化鲁棒调度成本，同时满足目标覆盖要求。通过将覆盖约束转化为形状相关半径，将原始双层问题转化为单阶段可微目标函数，利用SCED求解的KKT/对偶信息提供任务梯度而无需通过求解器进行微分。对于未知分布，采用四重训练/调优/校准/测试分割，结合平滑分位数敏感性估计器进行训练，使用分割共形校准进行部署。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点系统上评估，包含区域间传输约束的耦合SCED公式。学习的静态椭球相对于样本协方差基线减少了约4.8%的调度成本，同时保持经验覆盖高于目标水平。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种从调度任务本身学习储备需求相关性结构的方法，能够显著降低调度成本同时保证覆盖要求，并且相同的任务梯度可以传递给上下文相关编码器进行扩展应用。

Abstract: Operating reserve requirements in security-constrained economic dispatch (SCED) depend strongly on the assumed correlation structure of renewable forecast errors, yet that structure is usually specified exogenously rather than learned for the dispatch task itself. This paper formulates correlated reserve-set design as an end-to-end trainable robust optimization problem: choose the ellipsoidal uncertainty-set shape to minimize robust dispatch cost subject to a target coverage requirement. By profiling the coverage constraint into a shape-dependent radius, the original bilevel problem becomes a single-stage differentiable objective, and KKT/dual information from the SCED solve provides task gradients without differentiating through the solver. For unknown distributions, a four-way train/tune/calibrate/test split combines a smoothed quantile-sensitivity estimator for training with split conformal calibration for deployment, yielding finite-sample marginal coverage under exchangeability and a consistent gradient estimator for the smoothed objective. The same task gradient can also be passed upstream to context-dependent encoders, which we report as a secondary extension. The framework is evaluated on the IEEE~118-bus system with a coupled SCED formulation that includes inter-zone transfer constraints. The learned static ellipsoid reduces dispatch cost by about 4.8\% relative to the Sample Covariance baseline while maintaining empirical coverage above the target level.

</details>


### [70] [From Nonsmooth Minima to Smooth Branches via Heat Kernel Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05372)
*Hyeontae Jo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了非光滑优化问题中热核正则化的二阶分析框架，证明了在特定条件下正则化Hessian矩阵保持渐近非退化，从而为基于延拓的非光滑优化分析提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学和工程优化问题涉及在极小值点处非光滑的目标函数。传统方法通过正则化追踪极小值分支，但对于非光滑函数，这种分支能否延续以及相关的延拓方程是否局部可解尚不清楚。需要建立严格的理论框架来分析正则化方法在非光滑优化中的有效性。

Method: 采用热核正则化方法，研究正则化目标函数的极小值分支。在全局增长条件和局部主导项形式为|x|^a（1≤a≤2）的假设下，首先证明正则化目标存在全局极小值，且极小值分支在自然热尺度O(√t)处局部化。然后分析正则化Hessian矩阵的渐近行为，证明其由原始目标函数的局部剖面决定。

Result: 1. 正则化目标函数存在全局极小值，极小值分支在热尺度O(√t)处局部化；2. 正则化Hessian的渐近行为：a=2时保持一致正定，1≤a<2时最小特征值以受控速率t^{(a-2)/2}增长；3. 对于所有足够小的t>0，正则化Hessian保持渐近非退化，延拓方程保持局部可解，即使原始目标在极小值点处不存在经典Hessian。

Conclusion: 热核正则化在奇异极小值点附近恢复了非退化性，为基于延拓的非光滑优化分析提供了严格的二阶框架。该研究证明了正则化方法在非光滑优化中的理论有效性，即使原始目标函数在极小值点处不存在经典Hessian，正则化Hessian仍能保持渐近非退化，确保延拓方程的局部可解性。

Abstract: Many optimization problems in science and engineering involve objective functions that are nonsmooth at their minimizers. A common strategy is to trace a branch of minimizers of a regularized objective as the smoothing scale tends to zero; however, for nonsmooth functions, it is generally unclear whether such a branch can be continued and whether the associated continuation equation remains locally solvable. We study heat-kernel regularization and the resulting continuation equation along a local minimizing branch connected to a minimizer of the original objective. Under a global growth condition and a local leading-order description of the form $|x|^a$ with $1 \le a \le 2$, we first show that the regularized objective admits global minimizers and that any such minimizing branch localizes at the natural heat scale $O(\sqrt{t})$. We then prove that the asymptotic behavior of the regularized Hessian is determined by the local profile of the original objective: it remains uniformly positive definite in the quadratic case $a=2$, while in the subquadratic regime $1 \le a < 2$ its smallest eigenvalue grows at the controlled rate $t^{(a-2)/2}$. Consequently, the regularized Hessian remains asymptotically nondegenerate for all sufficiently small $t>0$, and the continuation equation remains locally solvable, even when the original objective does not admit a classical Hessian at the minimizer. Our results provide a rigorous second-order framework for continuation-based analysis in nonsmooth optimization by showing how heat regularization restores nondegeneracy near singular minimizers.

</details>


### [71] [Distributed Load Frequency Control of Multi-Area Smart Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05390)
*Wenjing Yang,Zhaorong Zhang,Xun Li,Juanjuan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种考虑信息隐私约束的多区域智能电网分布式负荷频率控制算法，通过分布式近似方法解决传统分布式最优控制方法在信息隐私保护下的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多区域智能电网中，传统分布式最优控制方法需要共享各区域的内部结构参数和运行状态，这违反了信息隐私保护要求。当考虑外部负荷扰动和测量噪声时，如何在保护各区域信息隐私的前提下实现全局最优功率调节成为一个重要挑战。

Method: 提出了一种分布式算法，通过分布式近似控制Riccati方程、估计Riccati方程和状态估计，在信息隐私约束下生成全局最优功率调节指令。该方法不要求各区域与非相邻区域共享内部参数和状态信息。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的分布式算法能够近似集中式最优控制的性能，并且在所提出的分布式控制器下的性能指标小于常用分布式控制方法的性能指标。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了信息隐私约束下的多区域智能电网分布式负荷频率控制问题，提出的分布式算法在保护各区域信息隐私的同时，能够实现接近集中式最优控制的性能，为智能电网的安全高效运行提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the distributed load frequency control problem in a multi-area smart grid under external load disturbances and measurement noise. The novelty lies in that the information privacy is fully taken into account, that is, the internal structural parameters and operational states of each area are not shared with non-neighboring areas, which makes traditional distributed optimal control methods ineffective. The main contribution is to propose a distributed algorithm for the global optimal power regulation command under information privacy constraints via distributed approximation of the control Riccati equation, the estimation Riccati equation, and the state estimation. Simulation results show that the proposed algorithm can approximate the performance of centralized optimal control, and the performance index under the proposed distributed controller is smaller than that under the commonly used distributed control.

</details>


### [72] [An Actor-Critic Framework for Continuous-Time Jump-Diffusion Controls with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05398)
*Liya Guo,Ruimeng Hu,Xu Yang,Yi Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于演员-评论家框架的网格自由求解器，用于处理具有时间非齐次跳跃扩散动态的熵正则化控制问题和随机博弈


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间随机控制在金融和经济学中至关重要，但在显式时间依赖性、不连续冲击和高维情况下计算最优策略非常困难

Method: 基于时间非齐次小q函数和适当占有测度的演员-评论家框架，使用条件归一化流参数化演员以表示连续动作空间中的表达性随机策略

Result: 在时间非齐次线性二次控制、Merton投资组合优化和多智能体投资组合博弈中验证了方法，数值结果显示在跳跃不连续性下稳定学习，准确逼近最优随机策略，并在维度和智能体数量方面具有良好的扩展性

Conclusion: 该方法为具有时间非齐次跳跃扩散动态的熵正则化控制问题和随机博弈提供了有效的网格自由求解器，能够处理复杂的时间依赖性和不连续性

Abstract: Continuous-time stochastic control with time-inhomogeneous jump-diffusion dynamics is central in finance and economics, but computing optimal policies is difficult under explicit time dependence, discontinuous shocks, and high dimensionality. We propose an actor-critic framework that serves as a mesh-free solver for entropy-regularized control problems and stochastic games with jumps. The approach is built on a time-inhomogeneous little q-function and an appropriate occupation measure, yielding a policy-gradient representation that accommodates time-dependent drift, volatility, and jump terms. To represent expressive stochastic policies in continuous-action spaces, we parameterize the actor using conditional normalizing flows, enabling flexible non-Gaussian policies while retaining exact likelihood evaluation for entropy regularization and policy optimization. We validate the method on time-inhomogeneous linear-quadratic control, Merton portfolio optimization, and a multi-agent portfolio game, using explicit solutions or high-accuracy benchmarks. Numerical results demonstrate stable learning under jump discontinuities, accurate approximation of optimal stochastic policies, and favorable scaling with respect to dimension and number of agents.

</details>


### [73] [Distributed Algorithm for the Global Optimal Controller of Nonlinear Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05443)
*Ruixue Li,Wenjing Yang,Zhaorong Zhang,Xun Li,Juanjuan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对非线性多智能体系统的分布式最优控制算法，解决了状态和系统动态结构私有且仅能在通信智能体间共享的隐私保护问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业保密等场景中，多智能体系统的状态和动态结构通常是私有的，只能在与通信的智能体之间共享。这种信息结构使得传统使用所有系统动态结构的分布式控制方法失效，需要新的解决方案。

Method: 通过分布式近似Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，提出了在这种实际信息结构下的全局最优控制器分布式算法。

Result: 实际数值仿真验证了所提算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了非线性多智能体系统在隐私保护信息结构下的分布式最优控制问题，提出的算法在实际应用中具有有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the distributed optimal control problem for a kind of nonlinear multi-agent systems. In particular,both the state and the system dynamic structures of each agent are private and can only be shared among communicating agents.This type of information structure is inevitable in fields such as collaborative control for industrial confidentiality, and renders traditional distributed control methods using all systems' dynamic structures ineffective. The primary contribution is the proposal of a distributed algorithm for the global optimal controller under such practical information structure via distributed approximation of the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. Practical numerical simulation demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [74] [A flatness proof of the exponential turnpike phenomenon for linear-quadratic optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05511)
*Michel Fliess,Claude Lobry,Emmanuel Trélat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文从微分平坦性角度重新审视有限维线性二次最优控制，将最优控制问题转化为常系数线性微分方程，揭示了turnpike现象的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性二次最优控制通常基于哈密顿-黎卡提分析，本文旨在从微分平坦性这一不同视角重新审视该问题，为turnpike现象提供新的解释机制。

Method: 利用可控线性系统的平坦性，将轨迹参数化为平坦输出及其有限阶导数。将参数化代入二次泛函后，欧拉-拉格朗日条件变为常系数线性微分方程。通过Smith标准形化简，方程解耦为标量常系数方程，解为指数多项式。

Result: 当约化欧拉-拉格朗日算子没有纯虚特征根且端点约束对稳定/不稳定模态非退化时，最优轨迹由左边界层、右边界层和长时间接近静态最优的内部弧组成。当权重半正定时，方程阶数可能降低，端点条件可能不兼容，多项式或振荡模态会破坏指数turnpike。

Conclusion: 微分平坦性视角为线性二次最优控制提供了不同于传统哈密顿-黎卡提分析的新理解，特别对turnpike现象给出了更直接的机制解释，并澄清了半正定权重情况下的变化。

Abstract: We revisit finite-dimensional linear-quadratic optimal control from the viewpoint of differential flatness. If the pair (A, B) is controllable, then the linear control system is flat, and every trajectory can be parametrized by a flat output and finitely many of its derivatives. Once this parametrization is inserted into the quadratic functional, the Euler-Lagrange condition becomes a linear differential equation with constant coefficients, or more generally a polynomial matrix differential equation. After reduction to Smith normal form, this equation decouples into scalar constant-coefficient equations, and its solutions are exponentialpolynomials. This yields a viewpoint on the turnpike phenomenon that is quite different from the classical Hamiltonian-Riccati analysis: the turnpike mechanism appears directly from the stable-unstable splitting of the reduced flat equation. In particular, when the reduced Euler-Lagrange operator has no purely imaginary characteristic roots and when the endpoint constraints act nondegenerately on the stable and unstable modes, the optimal trajectory consists of a left-boundary layer, a right-boundary layer, and a long interior arc exponentially close to the static optimum. The same viewpoint also clarifies what changes when some weights are only semidefinite: the order of the reduced equation may drop, some endpoint conditions may become incompatible, and polynomial or oscillatory modes may destroy the exponential turnpike. It also gives a natural meaning to certain endpoint constraints on the control and on finitely many derivatives of the control: such traces are not defined on the ambient L 2 control space, but they are meaningful on the smooth extremals selected by the reduced Euler-Lagrange equation. We formulate this principle as a general theorem and illustrate it in detail on the double integrator.

</details>


### [75] [Optimal Centered Active Excitation in Linear System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05518)
*Kaito Ito,Alexandre Proutiere*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最优中心噪声激励的线性系统辨识主动学习算法，该算法通过普通最小二乘和半定规划实现最小样本复杂度，并能高效计算系统矩阵估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在系统辨识中，主动学习算法需要平衡样本复杂度和计算效率。现有方法往往无法同时达到理论最优的样本复杂度和实际可行的计算效率，特别是在线性系统辨识场景下。

Method: 提出基于普通最小二乘（OLS）和半定规划（SDP）的主动学习算法，采用最优中心噪声激励策略。首先建立任意主动学习算法达到指定精度和置信水平所需样本复杂度的下界，然后推导所提算法的样本复杂度上界。

Result: 所提算法的样本复杂度上界与任意算法的下界匹配（相差通用因子），证明了算法的最优性。紧致的边界易于解释，并明确显示了其对状态维度等系统参数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该算法在理论上达到了最小样本复杂度，同时保持了计算效率，为线性系统辨识提供了理论最优且实用的主动学习解决方案。

Abstract: We propose an active learning algorithm for linear system identification with optimal centered noise excitation. Notably, our algorithm, based on ordinary least squares and semidefinite programming, attains the minimal sample complexity while allowing for efficient computation of an estimate of a system matrix. More specifically, we first establish lower bounds of the sample complexity for any active learning algorithm to attain the prescribed accuracy and confidence levels. Next, we derive a sample complexity upper bound of the proposed algorithm, which matches the lower bound for any algorithm up to universal factors. Our tight bounds are easy to interpret and explicitly show their dependence on the system parameters such as the state dimension.

</details>


### [76] [Scaled Graph Containment for Feedback Stability: Soft-Hard Equivalence and Conic Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05567)
*Eder Baron-Prada,Julius P. J. Krebbekx,Adolfo Anta,Florian Dörfler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为缩放图(SGs)开发了乘子定义区域内的包含条件，证明了圆形区域中软硬SG包含的等价性，实现了仅通过软计算进行硬稳定性认证，并识别了双曲凸的锥形区域以获得更紧的SG边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反馈稳定性分析方法存在计算复杂性和约束限制的问题，需要开发更高效的SG包含条件来简化稳定性认证过程，特别是避免现有方法中的正半定存储约束和同伦条件。

Method: 为缩放图(SGs)开发乘子定义区域内的包含条件，包括圆形和锥形几何。证明当相关乘子为正负时，圆形区域的软硬SG包含等价。识别双曲凸的锥形区域，这是频域证书所需的条件。

Result: 圆形包含框架在高达300状态的系统上实现了15-44%的计算节省。双曲凸锥形区域在算子SG非对称时提供了比圆形更紧的SG边界。软硬包含等价性使得仅通过软计算就能进行硬稳定性认证。

Conclusion: 该论文为缩放图稳定性分析提供了高效的包含条件，通过软硬包含等价性简化了认证过程，并识别了双曲凸锥形区域以获得更紧的边界，为反馈稳定性分析提供了实用的几何框架。

Abstract: Scaled graphs (SGs) offer a geometric framework for feedback stability analysis. This paper develops containment conditions for SGs within multiplier-defined regions, addressing both circular and conic geometries. For circular regions, we show that soft and hard SG containment are equivalent whenever the associated multiplier is positive-negative. This enables hard stability certification from soft computations alone, bypassing both the positive semidefinite storage constraint and the homotopy condition of existing methods. Numerical experiments on systems with up to 300 states demonstrate computational savings of 15-44 % for the circular containment framework. We further characterize which conic regions are hyperbolically convex, a condition our frequency-domain certificate requires, and demonstrate that such regions provide tighter SG bounds than circles whenever the operator SG is nonsymmetric.

</details>


### [77] [Consensus-based optimization with $α$-stable jump processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05626)
*Pedro Aceves-Sanchez,Giacomo Albi,Federica Ferrarese,Michael Herty*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合α稳定随机过程的CBO方法变体，通过非局部随机效应增强探索能力，建立了严格的收敛性结果并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基于扩散的优化方法在复杂优化场景中探索能力有限，需要引入非局部随机效应来改善探索性能，α稳定随机过程能提供更丰富的探索行为。

Method: 在动力学框架中引入α稳定随机过程的跳跃机制，在粒子层面构建随机动力学模型，通过傅里叶表示推导相关的分数阶Fokker-Planck方程，建立严格的收敛性分析。

Result: 建立了方法的严格收敛性理论，数值实验表明α稳定跳跃过程在复杂优化设置中比标准基于扩散的方法具有潜在优势，特别是在探索能力方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 提出的α稳定CBO方法通过非局部扩散行为有效增强了优化算法的探索能力，为复杂优化问题提供了有前景的解决方案，在理论和实验层面都证明了其有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel variant of the CBO method that incorporates jumps according to an $α$-stable stochastic process in a kinetic framework. This extension gives rise to nonlocal stochastic effects, which improve the exploration capabilities of the method. We formulate the method at the particle level, detailing the corresponding stochastic dynamics and its asymptotic behavior. In particular, through a Fourier-based representation, we derive the associated fractional Fokker-Planck equation, which naturally accounts for the nonlocal diffusion behaviors induced by $α$-stable processes. As a central result, we establish a rigorous convergence result for the proposed approach. Finally, we evaluate the performance of the method through a set of numerical experiments. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the $α$-stable jump process and emphasize its potential advantages over standard diffusion-based methods, particularly in complex optimization settings.

</details>


### [78] [Intrinsic perturbation scale for certified oracle objectives with epigraphic information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05678)
*Karim Bounja,Boujemaâ Achchab,Abdeljalil Sakat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于认证包络信息的自然位移控制方法，用于处理具有认证上镜图信息的oracle目标函数的最小化集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于目标函数扰动的局部一致值控制方法需要额外结构假设且无法从有限点信息中获得认证，需要更自然的控制方法

Method: 用柱状局部垂直上镜图控制替代传统局部一致值控制，该方法自然由认证包络提供，在集合二次增长条件下实现位移估计

Result: 在集合二次增长条件下（允许非唯一最小化点），获得了经典平方根位移估计，具有最优指数1/2，无需任何外部假设

Conclusion: 提出的基于认证包络的位移控制方法比传统方法更弱但更自然，在适当条件下能获得最优位移估计，为处理oracle目标函数的最小化集提供了新框架

Abstract: We introduce a natural displacement control for minimizer sets of oracle objectives equipped with certified epigraphic information. Formally, we replace the usual local uniform value control of objective perturbations - uncertifiable from finite pointwise information without additional structure - by the strictly weaker requirement of a cylinder-localized vertical epigraphic control, naturally provided by certified envelopes. Under set-based quadratic growth (allowing nonunique minimizers), this yields the classical square-root displacement estimate with optimal exponent 1/2, without any extrinsic assumption.

</details>


### [79] [A Posteriori Second-Order Guarantees for Bolza Problems via Collocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05811)
*Dongzhe Zheng,Wenjie Mei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个后验认证框架，用于验证直接配点法求解Bolza最优控制问题得到的离散KKT解是否满足连续二阶充分条件，通过重构轨迹、评估残差和计算下界来实现可操作的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接配点法产生离散KKT点，但实际求解器只输出离散量（原始-对偶迭代、约化Hessian、Jacobian等），这导致连续二阶最优性理论与求解器输出可验证内容之间存在差距。

Method: 从离散KKT解出发，重构分段多项式状态、控制和协态轨迹，评估动力学、边界和驻点条件的残差，推导连续二阶变分的可计算下界（表示为离散约化曲率减去显式的残差相关修正项）。

Result: 开发出可操作的后验认证框架，能够从配点输出验证连续二阶充分性，提供与局部增长和信赖域尺寸相关的定量信息，认证不等式中的常数可以从重构的离散数据保守估计。

Conclusion: 该认证框架填补了连续最优控制理论与离散求解器输出之间的理论-实践鸿沟，支持通过残差分解进行自适应网格细化，并可扩展到具有孤立横向切换的路径不等式问题。

Abstract: Direct collocation for Bolza optimal control yields discrete Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points, while practical solvers expose only discrete quantities such as primal-dual iterates, reduced Hessians, and Jacobians. This creates a gap between continuous second-order optimality theory and what can be certified from solver output. We develop an a posteriori certification framework that bridges this gap. Starting from a discrete KKT solution, we reconstruct piecewise polynomial state, control, and costate trajectories, evaluate residuals of the dynamics, boundary, and stationarity conditions, and derive a computable lower bound for the continuous second variation. The bound is expressed as the discrete reduced curvature minus explicit residual-dependent correction terms. A positive bound yields a sufficient certificate for continuous second-order sufficiency and provides quantitative information relevant to local growth and trust-region sizing. The constants entering the certification inequality are conservatively estimable from reconstructed discrete data. The resulting test is operationally verifiable from collocation outputs and naturally supports adaptive mesh refinement through residual decomposition.
